## Summary

## Strengths:

- Low prevalence of depression among children
- Low prevalence of students experiencing homelessness
- High prevalence of cervical cancer screening

## Highlights:

HPV VACCINATION

## Challenges:

- High child mortality rate
- High teen birth rate
- High prevalence of illicit drug use among women

## Delaware

State Health Department Website: dhss.delaware.gov

## Summary

## Strengths:

- Low prevalence of multiple chronic conditions among women
- Low prevalence of depression among children
- High supply of pediatricians

## Highlights:

DRUG DEATHS

83%

from 26.1 to 47.7 deaths per 100,000 women ages 20-44 between 2014-2016 and 2018-2020.

## Challenges:

- High prevalence of asthma among children
- Low WIC coverage among eligible children ages 0-4
- High incidence of chlamydia among women

PHYSICAL INACTIVITY

25%

from 29.5% to 22.1% of women ages 18-44 between 2016-2017 and 2019-2020.

HEALTH OF WOMEN AND CHILDREN REPORT 2022

State Health Department Website: floridahealth.gov

## Summary

## Strengths:

- Low prevalence of multiple chronic conditions among women
- Low prevalence of obesity among women
- Low prevalence of depression among children

## Highlights:

CIGARETTE SMOKING

<!-- image -->

from 16.7% to 11.9% of women ages 18-44 between 2014-2015 and 2019-2020.

## Challenges:

- Low flu vaccination coverage among women
- Low prevalence of preventive dental care among children
- Low percentage of infants exclusively breastfed for six months

SEVERE MATERNAL MORBIDITY

<!-- image -->

from 72.4 to 81.8 complications per 10,000 delivery hospitalizations between 2016 and 2019.

## Massachusetts

## Summary

## Strengths:

- Low child mortality rate
- Low prevalence of adverse childhood experiences (ACEs)
- High percentage of female college graduates

## Highlights:

TEEN BIRTHS

<!-- image -->

from 11.9 to 6.1 births per 1,000 females ages 15-19 between 2013 and 2020.

## Challenges:

- High prevalence of anxiety among children
- High prevalence of illicit drug use among youth
- Low prevalence of cervical cancer screening

## Michigan

## Summary

## Strengths:

-  High WIC coverage among eligible children ages 0-4
-  High prevalence of women with a dedicated health care provider
-  Low prevalence of physical inactivity among women

## Highlights:

FIREARM DEATHS

<!-- image -->

from 4.3 to 5.7 deaths per 100,000 females ages 20-44 between 20132015 and 2018-2020.

## Challenges:

-  High prevalence of frequent mental distress among women
-  High prevalence of cigarette smoking among women
-  High prevalence of household smoke

25%

from 8.0 to 6.0 diagnoses per 1,000 birth hospitalizations between 2017 and 2019.

## Strengths:

-  High percentage of youth who are flourishing
-  High prevalence of physical activity among children
-  Low prevalence of insufficient sleep among women

DRUG DEATHS

## Challenges:

-  High prevalence of excessive drinking among women
-  High racial disparity between Black and white children in poverty
-  Low percentage of children with adequate and continuous health insurance

HEALTH OF WOMEN AND CHILDREN REPORT 2022

## New Hampshire

State Health Department Website: dhhs.nh.gov

## Summary

## Strengths:

-  Low infant mortality rate
-  High prevalence of reading, singing or storytelling
-  Low percentage of household food insecurity

## Highlights:

OBESITY

<!-- image -->

from 22.1% to 30.1% of women ages 18-44 between 2013-2014 and 2019-2020.

## Challenges:

-  High prevalence of alcohol use among youth
-  High racial disparity between American Indian/Alaska Native and white children in poverty
-  High prevalence of frequent physical distress among women

CIGARETTE SMOKING

<!-- image -->

from 23.2% to 16.3% of women ages 18-44 between 2016-2017 and 2019-2020.

## Oklahoma

State Health Department Website: oklahoma.gov/health

## Summary

## Strengths:

- Low prevalence of excessive drinking among women
- Low prevalence of illicit drug use among youth
- Low Black/white residential segregation

## Highlights:

TEEN BIRTHS

<!-- image -->

from 42.9 to 25.0 births per 1,000 females ages 15-19 between 2013 and 2020.

## Challenges:

- High prevalence of adverse childhood experiences (ACEs)
- High percentage of household food insecurity
- High prevalence of physical inactivity among women

CERVICAL CANCER SCREENING

from 81.9% to 73.6% of women ages 21-44 between 2016 and 2020.

## Summary

## Strengths:

- Low infant mortality rate
- High prevalence of adequate sleep among children
- Low prevalence of physical inactivity among women

## Highlights:

## Women

- Low percentage of youth who are flourishing
- High prevalence of students experiencing homelessness
- Low prevalence of cervical cancer screening
